    "05451c659d5925e5f5df30a879749fb2bd379d4c9e4eafa8ab2790c8b3292434": {
        "ubp_id": "WORD_ENTROPY_001",
        "name": "Entropy",
        "math": "S = -k * sum(p * log(p))",
        "language": "A measure of disorder or randomness in a system; the tendency toward equilibrium.",
        "script": "entropy = -sum(p * log(p) for p in probabilities)",

            "information"

    "206fd26ff96e7bd9b50e41cee7793838f0e74d19892f2dda037635ab1ddebb94": {
        "ubp_id": "WORD_SYMMETRY_001",
        "name": "Symmetry",
        "math": "T(x) = x | Invariance under transformation",
        "language": "A property where an object remains unchanged under a set of transformations.",
        "script": "is_symmetric = all(transform(x) == x for transform in transforms)",
        "tags": [
            "symmetry",
            "invariance",
            "transformation",
            "geometry"
        ],
        "nrci": "1/1",
        "fingerprint": "206fd26ff96e7bd9b50e41cee7793838f0e74d19892f2dda037635ab1ddebb94"

    "4486f884a13c08ba7f80722703b16e963f4add2e37fd65a44057773bc34602d2": {
        "ubp_id": "WORD_RESONANCE_001",
        "name": "Resonance",
        "math": "f_resonant = f_natural | Amplitude -> max",
        "language": "The phenomenon where a system oscillates with maximum amplitude at specific frequencies.",
        "script": "resonance = abs(freq - natural_freq) < threshold",
        "tags": [
            "resonance",
            "frequency",

        "nrci": "1/1",
        "fingerprint": "4486f884a13c08ba7f80722703b16e963f4add2e37fd65a44057773bc34602d2"

    "a8de16525f2f81d703c785325af5bb8d1e3a5b98733320eb67d5da34522fe758": {
        "ubp_id": "WORD_COHERENCE_001",
        "name": "Coherence",
        "math": "phi_1 - phi_2 = constant",
        "language": "A fixed phase relationship between waves; the quality of forming a unified whole.",
        "script": "coherence = abs(phase1 - phase2) < epsilon",
        "tags": [

            "phase",
            "unity",

    "e871f171027e022ff291bae1357531a2f8bcae2a69be344b97705b9822318bce": {
        "ubp_id": "WORD_EMERGENCE_001",
        "name": "Emergence",
        "math": "Whole > sum(Parts)",
        "language": "Complex patterns arising from simple rules; properties that exist only at system level.",
        "script": "emergence = system_property not in [p for part in parts for p in part.properties]",
        "tags": [
            "emergence",

        "nrci": "1/1",

    "02e4681c01d9e0678ad7d44ea7108a9a2b0b66d3ae6528147096b2de23035cae": {
        "ubp_id": "WORD_RECURSION_001",
        "name": "Recursion",
        "math": "f(n) = g(f(n-1))",
        "language": "A process that refers to itself; self-similar structure at different scales.",
        "script": "def recurse(n): return base if n == 0 else f(recurse(n-1))",
        "tags": [
            "recursion",
            "self-reference",
            "fractal",
            "iteration"
        ],
        "nrci": "1/1",

        "ubp_id": "WORD_ABSTRACTION_001",
        "name": "Abstraction",
        "math": "Map: Concrete -> Essential",
        "language": "The process of removing details to focus on essential characteristics.",
        "script": "abstract = lambda concrete: {k: v for k, v in concrete.items() if k in essential}",

        "ubp_id": "WORD_INVARIANT_001",

        "math": "f(T(x)) = f(x) for all T",
        "language": "A property that remains unchanged under specified transformations.",
